- **grill-the-future**: Interrogate a plan, strategy, roadmap, pitch or spec for the bets it silently makes about the future — timelines, adoption, regulation, substitutes, "this stays true" — and score each by fragility. - **grill-the-implementation**: Use when a plan is agreed, tickets are next, and how it is built is still open: how systems talk (API, webhook, event, queue), where data lives, auth, what happens on failure, how it ships.
